wallet-service — Use Cases

The system of record for every party that holds a wallet in Bits Pay — and for whether each wallet may move money.
SVC-03 · modules wallet + partner · Source: docs/SPEC.md (v6) + docs/contracts (contractVersion 1.0.0) · For new developers · Example values are illustrative

All ids, amounts, names and timestamps in the examples are made up. Field names, paths, event types and codes are copied from the contracts. The SPEC's own examples use the agent "Salma Telecom" and the merchant "Nirob Store"; this guide renames them Mitu Store and Dhaka Bazar (QR payloads were re-encoded with a fresh CRC so they stay valid). Customers are Rahim, Karim and Fatema; staff are Sumon (maker), Tania (checker), Rafiq (sales) and Nasrin (compliance). Money is in BDT; on the wire it is always an integer number of poisha (100 poisha = 1 Tk).

1. What this service does, in one minute

Analogy. Think of wallet-service as the passport office and border gate list of Bits Pay. It registers people and businesses, checks their documents (KYC = "know your customer"), issues each one a wallet (the passport), and keeps a public, always-up-to-date list at the border (the Redis status cache) saying for every passport: "may travel out" (debit) and "may come in" (credit). It never carries anyone's money — the bank vault (ledger-service) does that.

Module wallet owns

  • Customers: profile (Bangla and English names, NID stored encrypted + hashed, DOB, addresses as GEO codes), language, nominees, versioned consents.
  • KYC submissions with save & resume, encrypted documents, de-duplication, blacklist check, screening hand-off, a manual review queue with maker-checker, tiers 0–2 and risk grades.
  • Wallets for every owner type (CUSTOMER, AGENT, MERCHANT, DISTRIBUTOR, INSTITUTION), always created ledger account first.
  • The wallet lifecycle: restrictions (freezes and blocks), dormancy, re-KYC, reactivation, closure with a same-person payout, MSISDN cooling, unclaimed-balance report, bulk pre-registration.
  • The wallet status cache in Redis (writer, loader, consistency sampler) — the data contract wallet-status-cache v1.

Module partner owns

  • Every non-customer wallet holder: agents, merchants, distributors and institutions.
  • Onboarding applications with checklists, key persons, bank accounts, site visits and configurable approval chains with SLAs.
  • The activation saga (wallet, category code, static QR, notification).
  • The effective-dated 5-level distribution hierarchy and the partner lifecycle (suspend, reactivate, terminate).
  • Bangla QR (EMVCo merchant-presented QR): static and dynamic codes, printing, on-us decoding.
  • Outlets, partner users and agreements (records, R2).

Both modules live in one Spring Boot service and talk to each other in-process only through Java facades (WalletFacade, PartnerFacade) — never through each other's tables. One shared kernel (common) holds the maker-checker engine, the outbox, idempotency store, field cipher and HTTP clients. Ports 8103 (HTTP) / 9103 (management); database schema svc_wallet.

This service must NEVER (SPEC §1.4)
  1. Talk to TigerBeetle or change a balance itself. Ledger calls are limited to account create / close, balance and holds reads, batch balances, the closure payout command (17) and its outcome lookup.
  2. Read another service's schema, or write Redis keys other than ws:*, wd:*, wlk:*, wsmeta:* and DEL wsn:*.
  3. Publish WALLET_UPSERTED for a wallet whose ledger account does not exist yet (provisioning_state ≠ READY).
  4. Publish any eventType other than PARTNER_UPSERTED on partner.events (compacted topic: a second type would overwrite partner state).
  5. Approve KYC without screening CLEAR (or decided CLEAR), blacklist done and no open exception; activate a partner whose screening is not CLEAR or whose blacklist check is not clean.
  6. Lift an AML / fraud / court / regulatory restriction without the authority in BR-W-12, or a freeze without the exact (reasonCode, reference) of its add.
  7. Close a wallet, pay out or anonymise on an unknown dependency state (ledger balance / holds, legal holds): refuse or wait.
  8. Log NID, full MSISDN, bank account numbers, document bytes, decrypted KYC data or dynamic QR payloads; store NID / account numbers in clear.
  9. Send a clear NID or account number in a URL (blacklist checks always use valueHash).
  10. Write status_code directly: the effective status always comes from StatusResolver (base status + restrictions).

Out of scope for now (SPEC §1.3): OTP / PIN / device binding (edge layer), OCR / EC NID check / liveness (every KYC is reviewed manually), minors, more than one CUSTOMER wallet per customer, bank / MFS closure payout, off-us QR payments, merchant bank settlement, geo-fencing, partner portal credentials.

2. Where it sits

Callers on the left use our REST API; on the right are the services we call and the topics we publish. Solid lines are synchronous calls (REST or Redis), dashed lines are asynchronous Kafka events. Numbers refer to the table below.

#From → toKindWhat
1channels → wallet-serviceRESTCustomer, KYC, closure, partner and QR APIs, incl. POST /v1/qr/decode.
2disbursement-service → wallet-serviceRESTPOST /v1/customers:preRegister, POST /v1/wallets/lookup:batch, POST /v1/wallets/status:batchGet.
3pricing-service → wallet-serviceRESTPOST /v1/wallets/status:batchGet.
4support-service → wallet-serviceRESTPOST /v1/wallets/{walletId}/status-changes, flags, PATCH /v1/customers/{customerId}.
5support-service → wallet-serviceeventsupport.events (disputes and reversal requests, for closure pre-checks).
6compliance-service → wallet-serviceRESTWallet status-changes, POST /v1/partners/{partnerId}/status-changes, GET /v1/customers/{customerId}.
7compliance-service → wallet-serviceeventcase.events (screening results, risk regrade recommendations).
8transaction-service → wallet-serviceRESTGET /v1/wallets/{walletId}/status — the cache-miss fallback.
9transaction-service → wallet-serviceeventtxn.events (activity for dormancy).
10wallet-service → ledger-serviceRESTPOST /v1/ledger/accounts, :close, balance, holds, balances:batchGet, POST /v1/ledger/commands/post-closure-payout and its lookup.
11wallet-service → limits-risk-serviceRESTGET /v1/blacklist/check, GET /v1/limits/wallets/{walletId}/usage.
12limits-risk-service → wallet-serviceeventblacklist.events.
13wallet-service → admin-serviceRESTGET /v1/retention/legal-holds (R2) and GET /v1/refdata/{type} (bootstrap).
14wallet-service → admin-serviceeventWrites notification.requests, approval.events, audit.events (topics owned by admin-service).
15admin-service → wallet-serviceeventrefdata.events (GEO, MCC, REASON_CODE, BANK, SYSTEM_PARAM).
16wallet-service → RedisdataWrites the wallet status cache (wallet-status-cache v1) with a Lua compare-and-set.
17Redis → transaction-servicedataPayment services read sender and receiver status in one round trip.
18wallet-service → wallet.eventseventWALLET_UPSERTED; read by transaction-, compliance-, pricing-, finance-ops-service and by our own cache loader.
19wallet-service → customer.eventsevent7 customer / KYC event types; read by transaction-, compliance-, pricing-, finance-ops-service (and admin-service notification rules).
20wallet-service → partner.eventseventPARTNER_UPSERTED; read by transaction-, compliance-, pricing-, treasury-, finance-ops-service.

A wallet-service outage never blocks payments while Redis is up: payment services read the cache directly and only fall back to GET /v1/wallets/{walletId}/status on a miss.

3. Key concepts and glossary

TermMeaning
MSISDNA mobile phone number. Stored in canonical form 8801XXXXXXXXX (13 digits); inputs like 01711000000 or +8801711000000 are normalised first.
KYC and tier"Know your customer". Tier 0 = MOBILE_VERIFIED (just registered), 1 = SIMPLIFIED, 2 = FULL. The tier decides limits (owned by limits-risk-service). Requirements per tier live in the KYC_TIERS config document.
Base status vs effective statusBase status is the lifecycle stage (PENDING_KYC, ACTIVE, DORMANT, PENDING_CLOSURE, CLOSED). Restrictions (DEBIT_BLOCK, CREDIT_BLOCK, FULL_BLOCK) are layered on top. The single letter everyone sees — P A D X C F Q Z — is computed by StatusResolver.
Status lettersP pending KYC (no debit), A active, D dormant (credit only), X debit-blocked, C credit-blocked, F blocked, Q pending closure (closure payout only), Z closed.
Wallet versionA counter on the wallet row that goes up by exactly 1 on every change. It is the optimistic lock, the WALLET_UPSERTED.version and the Redis cache version — one number for all three.
Status cache and CASA Redis hash per bucket (ws:{bucket}) holding version:status:tier:type:category:flags per wallet. Every write uses a Lua compare-and-set (CAS): write only if the new version is higher. So old or duplicate writes can never win.
Write-through and read-repairAfter each database commit the API writes the new value to Redis (write-through). When a status is read from the database (cache miss) the value is written back to Redis asynchronously (read-repair).
Maker-checkerFour-eyes control: one person (maker) proposes, a different person (checker) with the right role approves. One shared engine and the /v1/approvals endpoints serve both modules. Approvals expire after 72 h.
Screeningcompliance-service checks names and IDs against sanctions / PEP / internal lists after we publish KYC_SUBMITTED or PARTNER_UPSERTED (PENDING_SCREENING). Its answer comes back on case.events.
PEP and EDDPEP = politically exposed person. EDD = enhanced due diligence, required for HIGH risk / PEP: a second approval level (COMPLIANCE_OFFICER / CAMLCO) and extra fields.
Blacklist and valueHashlimits-risk-service keeps the blacklist. We always query it by an HMAC hash (valueHash) of the NID, MSISDN or bank account — never the clear value.
Tipping-offTelling a customer they are under AML investigation is illegal. So holders see RESTRICTED instead of AML_FREEZE / REGULATORY_INSTRUCTION / COURT_ORDER, and get no notification for them.
Outbox / inboxEvents are written to an outbox table in the same transaction as the business change and published afterwards — so a change and its event never disagree. Consumers record each eventId in an inbox (processed_event) so a redelivered event is applied once.
Compacted topicA Kafka topic that keeps at least the latest message per key (here walletId / partnerId). wallet.events and partner.events are compacted, so a new consumer can rebuild the full current state from them.
Idempotency-KeyA header the caller sets so a retried request gives the stored answer instead of doing the work twice. Same key + different body → 409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT. Kept 7 days.
Problem+jsonThe standard error body: code (e.g. STATE_CONFLICT), an optional machine reason (e.g. WALLET_CLOSED) and named extension members (e.g. changeRequestId).
PoishaThe smallest unit of BDT (1 Tk = 100 poisha). Amounts are long integers; QR amounts use BigDecimal; never floating point.
Partner, partner codeAny non-customer wallet holder. Its code is the type letter (A, M, D, I) + an 8-digit sequence + a Luhn check digit, e.g. A000045674.
Category codeAn integer (never an MCC) that tells pricing and limits what kind of partner a wallet is: agents 101–104, distributors 201–203, institutions 401–404, merchants 3000 + size×100 + MCC group (micro grocery = 3101). Customers = 0.
MCCMerchant category code (4 digits, e.g. 5411 grocery). Its group comes from refdata and feeds the category code.
Hierarchy, ancestorsOperator (level 0) → master distributor → distributor → sub-distributor → outlet (agent / merchant). ancestorPartnerIds lists the parents, nearest first. Assignments are effective-dated (valid from / to).
Bangla QR, EMVCo MPM, TLV, CRCMerchant-presented QR codes following EMVCo. The payload is a chain of TLV items (2-digit tag, 2-digit length, value) ending with tag 63, a CRC-16/CCITT-FALSE checksum. Our template is tag 26 with GUID BD.COM.BITSPAY.
Activation sagaA multi-step process (CREATE_WALLET → GENERATE_QR → FINALISE) whose progress is stored in a table, so a crash or a ledger outage just resumes later.
Settle windowAfter a closure is approved the wallet waits 15 minutes in Q so in-flight payments finish before the final balance check.
MSISDN coolingAfter a wallet closes, its phone number cannot be reused for 180 days.
DormancyA customer wallet with no outgoing activity for 365 days becomes DORMANT (D: it can still receive money).
UUIDv7 / UUIDv5v7 ids are time-ordered random ids (wallet ids, partner ids). v5 ids are derived deterministically from a name — used for the closure payout commandId so a retry produces the same id.
ShedLockA database lock that makes each scheduled job run in only one pod at a time.
Release R1 / R2R1 is the first release; R2 items (dormancy, re-KYC, pre-registration, unclaimed report, hierarchy, outlets, agreements) are designed and partly built now.

Wallet status codes (codes.yaml walletStatus)

CodeNameDebitCreditNotes
PPENDING_KYCFalsepolicydebit no; credit only when the reader's policy WALLET.ALLOW_INBOUND_BEFORE_KYC is true (default false)
AACTIVETrueTruedebit and credit
DDORMANTFalseTruecredit only
XDEBIT_BLOCKEDFalseTruecredit only
CCREDIT_BLOCKEDTrueFalsedebit only
FBLOCKEDFalseFalseno debit, no credit
QPENDING_CLOSUREclosure-onlyFalsedebit only by the closure payout (ledger command 17) or a treasury closure redemption (command 10, reasonCode CLOSURE_PAYOUT, partner wallets A/D/M/I); no credit
ZCLOSEDFalseFalseno debit, no credit; never deleted from the cache

Wallet flag bits (codes.yaml walletFlag)

BitNameNotes
1AML_WATCH
2PIN_RESET_COOLING
4PRE_REGISTERED
8HAS_OPEN_LIEN
16NEW_DEVICE_COOLINGreserved for the auth layer

Who are the actors?

Every request carries the kernel headers X-Actor-Type (CUSTOMER, AGENT, MERCHANT, DISTRIBUTOR, INSTITUTION, STAFF, SYSTEM, SERVICE), X-Actor-Id, optional X-Actor-Roles / X-Actor-User-Id, and X-Channel. Login and OTP happen at the edge; this service trusts these headers. support-service and compliance-service may forward a staff member's context (X-Actor-Type: SERVICE + the staff roles and user id); authority and masking are then evaluated on the forwarded roles. Staff roles you will meet: OPS_MAKER / OPS_CHECKER (KYC), SUP_AGENT / SUP_MAKER / SUP_CHECKER (support), COMPLIANCE_OFFICER, CAMLCO, RISK_ANALYST / RISK_CHECKER, PARTNER_OPS_MAKER / PARTNER_OPS_CHECKER, FIN_MAKER / FIN_CHECKER, AUDITOR, ADMIN (never a checker).

4. Features and use cases

Every one of the 102 REST operations in contracts/provided/openapi.yaml appears below as a card, grouped by feature. Each card gives the module tag (wallet, partner, approvals, internal) and release. Errors are copied from the contract; every operation can also answer the kernel 400 VALIDATION_FAILED and 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED. Open "Example request / response" for JSON whose field names match the schema.

Wallet core and status cache

Create wallets, read them, find them by phone number and keep the Redis status cache honest.

UC-001Create a wallet (ledger account first)walletR1
POST/v1/wallets
operationId createWallet

Dhaka Bazar has just passed its last approval stage. The activation saga (or, for a repair, an ADMIN) asks for a MERCHANT wallet for MSISDN 01812000111 with category 3101. wallet-service first opens the ledger account, then marks the wallet READY and answers 201 with status A. If the same call is repeated, the same walletId comes back with 200.

Triggered by
STAFF ADMIN (repair) or SYSTEM wallet-service. In practice the partner module calls the same logic in-process through WalletFacade.createWallet.
Rules
  • Ledger-first (ADR-05): the row is written as LEDGER_PENDING, ledger-service is called, then it becomes READY; a ledger outage answers 503 and the provisioning-retry job finishes it (backoff 10 s → 10 min, max 50).
  • Natural idempotency on (ownerType, ownerId, walletType): a repeat returns the existing wallet.
  • ownerType must match walletType (CUSTOMER↔C, AGENT↔A …). ownerType=CUSTOMER from outside → 403: customer wallets come only from registration.
  • MSISDN unique among non-CLOSED wallets (scope GLOBAL), not in the 180-day cooling period, and the owner may not already have that wallet type with another MSISDN (BR-W-19).
  • Partner wallets start A with kycTier = 2 (BR-W-01). WALLET_UPSERTED is published only once READY.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · malformed header, parameter or body
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 409 STATE_CONFLICT — reasons DUPLICATE_MSISDN, MSISDN_COOLING, OWNER_HAS_DIFFERENT_MSISDN · MSISDN uniqueness / cooling / owner already has another MSISDN
  • 503 DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE · ledger down: row kept LEDGER_PENDING, retry returns the same walletId
Emits
WALLET_UPSERTED (first event, when READY; previousStatus null); AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events (WALLET_CREATED)
Example request / response
Request
POST /v1/wallets
X-Actor-Type: STAFF
X-Actor-Id: 0192f1aa-0000-7000-8000-0000000000a1
X-Actor-Roles: ADMIN
X-Channel: BACKOFFICE
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "ownerType": "MERCHANT",
  "ownerId": "0192f4a0-0e01-7c00-8a00-00000000e001",
  "walletType": "M",
  "msisdn": "8801812000111",
  "categoryCode": 3101,
  "displayName": "Dhaka Bazar"
}
Response 201 — created
{
  "walletId": "0192f2dd-3a4b-7c5d-8e6f-708192a3b4c5",
  "status": "A"
}
Response 200 — already existed (same walletId)
{
  "walletId": "0192f2dd-3a4b-7c5d-8e6f-708192a3b4c5",
  "status": "A"
}
UC-002Read wallet detailwalletR1
GET/v1/wallets/{walletId}
operationId getWallet

support-service opens Karim's wallet on an agent's screen: owner, MSISDN, status A, tier 1, version 8812. If Karim himself calls it while under an AML freeze, he sees statusReasonCode: RESTRICTED instead of the real reason.

Triggered by
The holder itself (CUSTOMER / AGENT / MERCHANT / DISTRIBUTOR / INSTITUTION, own wallet), any STAFF, listed SERVICE callers, SYSTEM.
Rules
  • Only READY wallets exist for readers (404 otherwise). Served cache-aside from wd:{walletId}.
  • MSISDN masking by caller (BR-W-68). Tipping-off: holders get RESTRICTED for AML_FREEZE / REGULATORY_INSTRUCTION / COURT_ORDER.
  • A caller that keeps a copy of the contact overwrites it only when version is newer.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · malformed header, parameter or body
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · unknown or not READY
Emits
nothing (read-only)
Example request / response
Request
GET /v1/wallets/0192f2bb-4d5f-7c62-9b3e-6f1a4dac8b12
X-Actor-Type: CUSTOMER
X-Actor-Id: 0192f2a0-5b1c-7d10-8e11-0a1b2c3d4e01
X-Channel: APP
Response 200
{
  "walletId": "0192f2bb-4d5f-7c62-9b3e-6f1a4dac8b12",
  "ownerType": "CUSTOMER",
  "ownerId": "0192f2a0-5b1c-7d10-8e11-0a1b2c3d4e02",
  "msisdn": "8801711000000",
  "displayName": "Karim Ahmed",
  "language": "bn",
  "status": "A",
  "statusName": "ACTIVE",
  "statusReasonCode": "KYC_APPROVED",
  "kycTier": 1,
  "walletType": "C",
  "walletTypeName": "CUSTOMER",
  "categoryCode": 0,
  "flags": 0,
  "version": 8812,
  "createdAt": "2026-10-09T05:10:00.000Z",
  "updatedAt": "2026-10-09T06:00:00.000Z"
}
UC-003Status object (cache-miss fallback)walletR1
GET/v1/wallets/{walletId}/status
operationId getWalletStatus

transaction-service is about to let Rahim send 500 Tk to Karim. Redis has no field for Rahim's wallet (for example after a flush), so it calls this endpoint and gets version 8813, status A, kycTier 2 — the same six values that live in the cache.

Triggered by
SERVICE transaction-service, disbursement-service, pricing-service; SYSTEM; any STAFF.
Rules
  • Read from the primary database (never a replica), one primary-key lookup, no outbound call; p99 ≤ 20 ms.
  • After answering, a read-repair CAS of the same tuple is queued (bounded queue, drop on overflow — BR-W-53).
  • Not READY or unknown → 404; the reader may then write the negative-cache key wsn:{walletId} for 60 s.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · malformed header, parameter or body
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · unknown or not READY (the reader may write wsn:)
  • 503 DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE · database unavailable
Emits
nothing (read-only)
Example request / response
Request
GET /v1/wallets/0192f2bb-4d5f-7c62-9b3e-6f1a4dac8b12/status
X-Actor-Type: SERVICE
X-Actor-Id: transaction-service
X-Channel: API
Response 200
{
  "walletId": "0192f2aa-1c3e-7b51-8a2d-5e0f3c9b7a01",
  "version": 8813,
  "status": "A",
  "kycTier": 2,
  "walletType": "C",
  "categoryCode": 0,
  "flags": 0
}
UC-004Status objects for up to 1,000 walletswalletR1
POST/v1/wallets/status:batchGet
operationId batchGetWalletStatus

pricing-service prices a salary batch of 1,000 recipients. One call returns each wallet's status; one id that was never created comes back in notFound.

Triggered by
SERVICE disbursement-service, pricing-service, transaction-service; SYSTEM; any STAFF.
Rules
  • 1..1000 ids, duplicates ignored; one WHERE wallet_id = ANY(?) query on the primary.
  • notFound includes not-yet-READY wallets; callers treat them as not eligible and do not retry in the same call.
  • Read-repair CAS writes are queued for the values read.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · 0 or > 1000 ids, malformed uuid
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
Emits
nothing (read-only)
Example request / response
Request
POST /v1/wallets/status:batchGet
X-Actor-Type: SERVICE
X-Actor-Id: pricing-service
X-Channel: API
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "walletIds": [
    "0192f2aa-1c3e-7b51-8a2d-5e0f3c9b7a01",
    "0192f2bb-4d5f-7c62-9b3e-6f1a4dac8b12",
    "0192f2ff-0000-7000-8000-000000000999"
  ]
}
Response 200
{
  "items": [
    {
      "walletId": "0192f2aa-1c3e-7b51-8a2d-5e0f3c9b7a01",
      "version": 8813,
      "status": "A",
      "kycTier": 2,
      "walletType": "C",
      "categoryCode": 0,
      "flags": 0
    },
    {
      "walletId": "0192f2bb-4d5f-7c62-9b3e-6f1a4dac8b12",
      "version": 8812,
      "status": "A",
      "kycTier": 1,
      "walletType": "C",
      "categoryCode": 0,
      "flags": 0
    }
  ],
  "notFound": [
    "0192f2ff-0000-7000-8000-000000000999"
  ]
}
UC-005Find the wallet of a phone numberwalletR1
GET/v1/wallets/lookup
operationId lookupWallet

Rahim types Karim's number 01711000000 to send money. The app shows a confirmation screen "Karim A***" with status A before Rahim enters his PIN.

Triggered by
Holder actors (own wallet), SERVICE disbursement-service, treasury-service, support-service, any STAFF.
Rules
  • Accepts 01…, 880…, +880…; normalised to 8801XXXXXXXXX. walletType defaults to C.
  • Returns only a READY, non-CLOSED wallet (never Z). Name matching is not supported.
  • Masked name: customer wallets show first name + initials + ***; partner wallets show the full display name (BR-W-65). Cache-aside wlk: → wd:.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · malformed MSISDN or walletType
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · no READY non-CLOSED wallet
Emits
nothing (read-only)
Example request / response
Request
GET /v1/wallets/lookup?msisdn=01711000000&walletType=C
X-Actor-Type: CUSTOMER
X-Actor-Id: 0192f2a0-5b1c-7d10-8e11-0a1b2c3d4e01
X-Channel: APP
Response 200
{
  "walletId": "0192f2bb-4d5f-7c62-9b3e-6f1a4dac8b12",
  "displayNameMasked": "Karim A***",
  "walletType": "C",
  "status": "A"
}
UC-006Batch phone-number lookupwalletR1
POST/v1/wallets/lookup:batch
operationId batchLookupWallets

disbursement-service uploads a salary file from an employer with 2 numbers. Karim's number resolves to his wallet; 01755000017 has no wallet, so its item has walletId: null — not an error.

Triggered by
SERVICE disbursement-service; any STAFF.
Rules
  • 1..1000 MSISDNs; one item per MSISDN, same order, MSISDN echoed exactly as sent.
  • A malformed MSISDN gives a null item, never a 400. Not READY or CLOSED → walletId: null.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · 0 or > 1000 msisdns, invalid walletType
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
Emits
nothing (read-only)
Example request / response
Request
POST /v1/wallets/lookup:batch
X-Actor-Type: SERVICE
X-Actor-Id: disbursement-service
X-Channel: API
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "walletType": "C",
  "msisdns": [
    "8801711000000",
    "01755000017"
  ]
}
Response 200
{
  "items": [
    {
      "msisdn": "8801711000000",
      "walletId": "0192f2bb-4d5f-7c62-9b3e-6f1a4dac8b12",
      "walletType": "C",
      "status": "A",

  • Value must be one of codes.yaml categoryCode (44 values; merchants = 3000 + size×100 + MCC group).
  • Applied immediately; bumps version, keeps statusReasonCode.
Errors

UC-008Set / clear wallet flag bitswalletR1
POST/v1/wallets/{walletId}/flags
operationId changeWalletFlags

Karim sent 2,000 Tk to a wrong number and opened a ticket. support-service sets flag 8 (HAS_OPEN_LIEN) with reason WRONG_TRANSFER_HOLD. Later compliance also sets bit 8 for a lien; when support clears its own holder row, the bit stays on because compliance still holds it.

Triggered by
SERVICE compliance-service, support-service; SYSTEM; STAFF RISK_ANALYST, COMPLIANCE_OFFICER, SUP_MAKER.
Rules
  • Per-holder model (ADR-08, BR-W-17): holder = SERVICE X-Actor-Id, else backoffice. A bit stays set while any holder holds it; clear removes only the caller's own row.
  • A bit in both set and clear → 400. Bits 4 (PRE_REGISTERED) and 16 (NEW_DEVICE_COOLING) cannot be set from outside → 403.
  • reasonCode from the "Wallet flag" group or the ledger Hold codes (DISPUTE_HOLD, AML_LIEN, WRONG_TRANSFER_HOLD).
  • walletVersion is bumped only when the effective flags value changes. Immediate and audited.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · malformed header, parameter or body
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · bits 4 / 16, or role not allowed
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · path entity missing (or not visible to the actor)
  • 409 STATE_CONFLICT — reasons WALLET_CLOSED · closed wallet
Emits
WALLET_UPSERTED (only if the effective flags changed); AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events (WALLET_FLAGS_CHANGED)
Example request / response
Request
POST /v1/wallets/0192f2bb-4d5f-7c62-9b3e-6f1a4dac8b12/flags
X-Actor-Type: SERVICE
X-Actor-Id: support-service
X-Channel: API
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "set": 8,
  "clear": 0,
  "reasonCode": "WRONG_TRANSFER_HOLD"
}
Response 200
{
  "walletId": "0192f2bb-4d5f-7c62-9b3e-6f1a4dac8b12",
  "flags": 8,
  "walletVersion": 8814,
  "holders": [
    {
      "flag": 8,
      "holder": "support-service",
      "reasonCode": "WRONG_TRANSFER_HOLD"
    }
  ]
}

Wallet status changes and history

Freeze, block, unblock, set dormant, reactivate — and see who did what.

UC-009Add / lift a restriction, set dormant or reactivatewalletR1
POST/v1/wallets/{walletId}/status-changes
operationId changeWalletStatus

Two cases. (1) compliance-service freezes Karim's wallet for AML case CASE-2026-000045: applied at once, 200 APPLIED, status F. (2) Fatema phones the call centre because she lost her phone; agent Sumon (SUP_AGENT) requests BLOCKED with CUSTOMER_REQUEST: the answer is 202 PENDING_APPROVAL until a SUP_CHECKER approves.

Triggered by
STAFF SUP_AGENT, SUP_MAKER, OPS_MAKER, RISK_ANALYST, COMPLIANCE_OFFICER; SERVICE support-service, compliance-service, limits-risk-service, admin-service; SYSTEM wallet-service.
Rules
  • Target → operation (BR-W-10): BLOCKED → add FULL_BLOCK, DEBIT_BLOCKED → add DEBIT_BLOCK, CREDIT_BLOCKED → add CREDIT_BLOCK, ACTIVE → lift (or REACTIVATE if dormant and nothing matched), DORMANT → SET_DORMANT. PENDING_KYC / PENDING_CLOSURE / CLOSED → TARGET_NOT_ALLOWED.
  • Immediate only for SERVICE/SYSTEM in the immediate table (BR-W-11): compliance-service AML_FREEZE / FRAUD_FREEZE; the partner module's PARTNER_* reasons; system jobs. Everything else → WalletStatusChange / APPLY approval and 202.
  • Authority per reason (BR-W-12, REASON_AUTHORITY), e.g. AML_FREEZE maker COMPLIANCE_OFFICER / checker CAMLCO. Missing authority → 403.
  • reference mandatory for COURT_ORDER, REGULATORY_INSTRUCTION, AML_FREEZE, FRAUD_FREEZE on add and lift; a freeze is lifted only by the exact (reasonCode, reference) (BR-W-16).
  • Same active restriction → NOOP (200, nothing emitted); same request already pending → ALREADY_PENDING with changeRequestId (BR-W-13).
  • REACTIVATE needs KYC review not OVERDUE (REKYC_REQUIRED) and no open AML / court / regulatory freeze (OPEN_REGULATORY_FREEZE) (BR-W-33).
  • Every applied add or lift bumps the version and sets statusReasonCode, even if the letter stays the same. For freezes the response waits for the Redis write-through.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · unknown reason, missing reference on add or lift
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · no authority for the reason (REASON_AUTHORITY)
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · path entity missing (or not visible to the actor)
  • 409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT / STATE_CONFLICT — reasons ALREADY_PENDING, OPEN_REGULATORY_FREEZE, REKYC_REQUIRED, TARGET_NOT_ALLOWED, WALLET_CLOSED — extensions changeRequestId · changeRequestId carries the pending request when reason = ALREADY_PENDING; same key, different body
Emits
WALLET_UPSERTED (when applied); APPROVAL_REQUESTED on approval.events (when 202); AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events (WALLET_STATUS_CHANGE_REQUESTED / WALLET_STATUS_CHANGED)
Example request / response
Request
POST /v1/wallets/0192f2bb-4d5f-7c62-9b3e-6f1a4dac8b12/status-changes
X-Actor-Type: SERVICE
X-Actor-Id: compliance-service
X-Channel: API
Idempotency-Key: k-changeWalletStatus
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "targetStatus": "BLOCKED",
  "reasonCode": "AML_FREEZE",
  "reference": "CASE-2026-000045",
  "comment": "FREEZE CASE-2026-000045"
}
Response 200 — APPLIED or NOOP
{
  "changeRequestId": "0192f400-2222-7000-8000-00000000a002",
  "walletId": "0192f2bb-4d5f-7c62-9b3e-6f1a4dac8b12",
  "outcome": "APPLIED",
  "approvalId": null,
  "walletStatus": "F",
  "walletVersion": 8813
}
Response 202 — PENDING_APPROVAL
{
  "changeRequestId": "0192f400-1111-7000-8000-00000000a001",
  "walletId": "0192f2aa-1c3e-7b51-8a2d-5e0f3c9b7a01",
  "outcome": "PENDING_APPROVAL",
  "approvalId": "0192f400-1111-7000-8000-00000000b001",
  "walletStatus": "A",
  "walletVersion": 8813
}

Customers, profile, nominees and consents

Registration, search, profile reads and changes, nominees, consents and bulk pre-registration.

UC-012Register a customer (CUSTOMER wallet P, tier 0)walletR1
POST/v1/customers
operationId registerCustomer

Rahim registers at agent Mitu Store with number 01812000001 and accepts the Bangla terms and privacy policy. He gets a customer id and a CUSTOMER wallet in status P (pending KYC) at tier 0. If the app retries with the same number while he is still REGISTERED, it gets his existing record back (200 RESUMED).

Triggered by
CUSTOMER self (X-Channel APP / USSD), AGENT assisting, STAFF OPS_MAKER, SYSTEM. Idempotency-Key is required.
Rules
  • The MSISDN is assumed verified upstream (OTP deferred). TERMS and PRIVACY consents are mandatory (400 otherwise).
  • De-dup (BR-W-20): a live customer with this MSISDN → DUPLICATE_MSISDN; same REGISTERED customer → RESUMED; a PRE_REGISTERED one → linked (LINKED_PRE_REGISTRATION).
  • MSISDN in cooling (180 days after a closure) → MSISDN_COOLING.
  • Blacklisted MSISDN: registration is still allowed but blacklist_hit is set and the first KYC submission goes to EXCEPTION (BR-W-21).
  • Ledger-first wallet creation; on a ledger outage the answer is 503, the customer and wallet rows are kept and the retry job provisions the wallet (the walletId comes back on resume).
  • An assisting agent is stored as assistedBy; referralCode is opaque and only emitted on CUSTOMER_REGISTERED.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · MSISDN format, missing TERMS/PRIVACY consent
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT / STATE_CONFLICT — reasons DUPLICATE_MSISDN, MSISDN_COOLING · MSISDN used by another live customer / in cooling; same Idempotency-Key, different body
  • 503 DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE · ledger down: customer and wallet kept, wallet provisioned by the retry job (walletId returned on resume)
Emits
CUSTOMER_REGISTERED (reasonCode REGISTERED); WALLET_UPSERTED (status P, when READY); AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events (CUSTOMER_REGISTERED, CONSENT_GRANTED)
Example request / response
Request
POST /v1/customers
X-Actor-Type: AGENT
X-Actor-Id: 0192f4a0-0a01-7c00-8a00-00000000a001
X-Channel: PARTNER_APP
Idempotency-Key: k-registerCustomer
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "msisdn": "8801812000001",
  "language": "bn",
  "nameEn": "Rahim Uddin",
  "referralCode": null,
  "consents": [
    {
      "consentType": "TERMS",
      "documentVersion": "TNC-2026.10-bn",
      "language": "bn"
    },
    {
      "consentType": "PRIVACY",
      "documentVersion": "PRV-2026.10-bn",
      "language": "bn"
    }
  ]
}
Response 201
{
  "customerId": "0192f2a0-5b1c-7d10-8e11-0a1b2c3d4e01",
  "walletId": "0192f2aa-1c3e-7b51-8a2d-5e0f3c9b7a01",
  "status": "REGISTERED",
  "walletStatus": "P",
  "kycTier": 0,
  "result": "CREATED"
}
Response 200 — resumed or linked
{
  "customerId": "0192f2a0-5b1c-7d10-8e11-0a1b2c3d4e01",
  "walletId": "0192f2aa-1c3e-7b51-8a2d-5e0f3c9b7a01",
  "status": "REGISTERED",
  "walletStatus": "P",
  "kycTier": 0,
  "result": "RESUMED"
}

UC-015Change profile / contact (maker-checker)walletR1
PATCH/v1/customers/{customerId}
operationId updateCustomerProfile

Rahim changed his SIM. At a service point, Sumon (SUP_MAKER) requests the new number 01812000009 with ticket T261009000123. The answer is 202 PENDING_APPROVAL; when a SUP_CHECKER approves, both the customer and his wallet contact change.

Triggered by
STAFF OPS_MAKER, SUP_MAKER; SERVICE support-service (sends Idempotency-Key: sa-{actionId}).
Rules
  • Creates CustomerProfile / UPDATE (checker OPS_CHECKER or SUP_CHECKER). One pending per customer (ALREADY_PENDING).
  • NID-derived fields (NID, names, DOB, parents) are locked → FIELD_LOCKED (BR-W-38).
  • MSISDN uniqueness and cooling are checked at request and again at approval.
  • On approval: fields applied; an MSISDN change also updates the wallet contact and evicts the wlk: / wd: caches.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · malformed header, parameter or body
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · path entity missing (or not visible to the actor)
  • 409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT / STATE_CONFLICT — reasons ALREADY_PENDING, DUPLICATE_MSISDN, FIELD_LOCKED, MSISDN_COOLING — extensions changeRequestId · STATE_CONFLICT; same Idempotency-Key, different body
Emits
APPROVAL_REQUESTED on approval.events; On approval: CUSTOMER_PROFILE_UPDATED (PROFILE_UPDATED), WALLET_UPSERTED if the MSISDN changed; AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events

PUT/v1/customers/{customerId}/nominees
operationId replaceNominees

Rahim names his wife Ayesha Begum as nominee for 100 % (shareBps 10000). Before his first KYC approval it is saved at once (200); after approval the same change becomes a profile approval (202).

Triggered by
CUSTOMER (own, before first approval), AGENT assisting, STAFF OPS_MAKER, SUP_MAKER.
Rules
  • Σ shareBps must equal 10000 (100.00 %) → else 400.
  • Nominee ID numbers are stored encrypted; only idMasked is returned.
  • After the first KYC approval: maker-checker as CustomerProfile / UPDATE.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · shares do not sum to 10000
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · path entity missing (or not visible to the actor)
Emits
CUSTOMER_PROFILE_UPDATED (NOMINEES_UPDATED) when applied; APPROVAL_REQUESTED on approval.events (when 202); AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events (NOMINEES_UPDATED)
Example request / response
Request
PUT /v1/customers/0192f2a0-5b1c-7d10-8e11-0a1b2c3d4e01/nominees
X-Actor-Type: CUSTOMER
X-Actor-Id: 0192f2a0-5b1c-7d10-8e11-0a1b2c3d4e01
X-Channel: APP
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "nominees": [
    {
      "name": "Ayesha Begum",
      "relationship": "SPOUSE",
      "idType": "NID",
      "idNumber": "1990123451234",
      "shareBps": 10000,
      "dob": "1990-04-12"
    }
  ]
}
Response 200 — applied
{
  "items": [
    {
      "nomineeId": "0192f2d6-0000-7000-8000-0000000000a6",
      "name": "Ayesha Begum",
      "relationship": "SPOUSE",
      "idType": "NID",
      "idMasked": "******1234",
      "shareBps": 10000,
      "dob": "1990-04-12"
    }
  ]
}
Response 202 — pending approval
{
  "changeRequestId": "0192f400-1111-7000-8000-00000000a001",
  "approvalId": "0192f400-1111-7000-8000-00000000b001",
  "outcome": "PENDING_APPROVAL"
}

UC-021Withdraw a consentwalletR1
POST/v1/customers/{customerId}/consents/{consentId}:withdraw
operationId withdrawConsent

Fatema withdraws her MARKETING consent in the app. If she tried to withdraw TERMS, the answer would be 409 CONSENT_REQUIRED — to leave the terms she must close the wallet.

Triggered by
CUSTOMER (own); STAFF OPS_MAKER, SUP_MAKER, SUP_AGENT.
Rules
  • TERMS and PRIVACY cannot be withdrawn (CONSENT_REQUIRED); a second withdraw → ALREADY_WITHDRAWN.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · malformed header, parameter or body
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · path entity missing (or not visible to the actor)

UC-022Bulk pre-registration of salary / G2P recipients (R2)walletR2
POST/v1/customers:preRegister
operationId preRegisterCustomers

A garment factory (an INSTITUTION partner) will pay salaries through Bits Pay. disbursement-service pre-registers 1,000 workers. Shila Akter (row 17) is new: customer PRE_REGISTERED + wallet D with flag 4. Karim (row 18) already has a wallet: EXISTS.

Triggered by
SERVICE disbursement-service. Idempotency-Key required (prereg-{batchId}-{groupNo}).
Rules
  • Per row (BR-W-36): invalid MSISDN → REJECTED INVALID_MSISDN; live customer → EXISTS; cooling → REJECTED MSISDN_COOLING; only a CLOSED wallet still being closed → REJECTED CLOSED_WALLET; else CREATED.
  • Created wallets are base DORMANT (D: credit allowed, no debit) with flag 4 PRE_REGISTERED; KYC approval later makes them ACTIVE.
  • ≤ 1,000 rows, chunks of 200 per transaction; a 503 fails the whole request and creates nothing. Unknown fields are ignored.
  • A returned walletId is usable only once GET …/status or status:batchGet returns it (ledger provisioning may still be running).
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · malformed header, parameter or body
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT · same Idempotency-Key, different body
  • 503 DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE · whole request fails, nothing created
Emits
CUSTOMER_REGISTERED (PRE_REGISTRATION) per created row; WALLET_UPSERTED per wallet when READY; AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events
Example request / response
Request
POST /v1/customers:preRegister
X-Actor-Type: SERVICE
X-Actor-Id: disbursement-service
X-Channel: API
Idempotency-Key: prereg-0192f2f0-1
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "institutionId": "0192f2e0-0000-7000-8000-00000000e100",
  "rows": [
    {
      "rowRef": "17",
      "msisdn": "8801755000017",
      "nameEn": "Shila Akter",
      "nidLast4": "4421"
    },
    {
      "rowRef": "18",
      "msisdn": "8801711000000",
      "nameEn": "Karim Ahmed"
    }
  ]
}
Response 200
{
  "results": [
    {
      "rowRef": "17",
      "result": "CREATED",
      "reason": null,
      "customerId": "0192f500-0000-7000-8000-000000000017",
      "walletId": "0192f501-0000-7000-8000-000000000017",
      "status": "D"
    },
    {
      "rowRef": "18",
      "result": "EXISTS",
      "reason": null,
      "customerId": "0192f2a0-5b1c-7d10-8e11-0a1b2c3d4e02",
      "walletId": "0192f2bb-4d5f-7c62-9b3e-6f1a4dac8b12",
      "status": "A"
    }
  ],
  "created": 1,
  "existing": 1,
  "rejected": 0
}

KYC submissions and review

Capture KYC data and documents, submit, screen, review with maker-checker, handle exceptions.

UC-023Start a KYC submission (DRAFT)walletR1
POST/v1/customers/{customerId}/kyc-submissions
operationId createKycSubmission

Rahim wants full KYC (tier 2) to raise his limits. Mitu Store starts a DRAFT submission for targetTier 2; he has 7 days to finish it.

Triggered by
CUSTOMER (own), AGENT assisting, STAFF OPS_MAKER.
Rules
  • One open submission per customer (OPEN_SUBMISSION_EXISTS with existingId).
  • Upgrade: targetTier must be higher than the current tier (TIER_NOT_HIGHER, BR-W-29).
  • After maxKycAttempts (3) rejected attempts only STAFF may create (MAX_ATTEMPTS, BR-W-27).
  • resumeExpiresAt = now + 7 days (BR-W-30).
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · malformed header, parameter or body
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · path entity missing (or not visible to the actor)
  • 409 STATE_CONFLICT — reasons MAX_ATTEMPTS, OPEN_SUBMISSION_EXISTS, TIER_NOT_HIGHER — extensions existingId · existingId = the open submission
Emits
AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events

Triggered by

Rules
  • Only in DRAFT (NOT_DRAFT). Partial saves allowed; each save extends resumeExpiresAt by draftResumeDays (7).
  • Validation at capture (BR-W-60..64): canonical MSISDN, NID 10/13/17 digits, nameBn only Bangla letters, age 18..120, GEO codes as a parent chain.
  • The clear NID is never returned: responses show nidMasked.

UC-025Upload a KYC documentwalletR1
POST/v1/kyc-submissions/{submissionId}/documents
operationId uploadKycDocument

Mitu Store photographs the front of Rahim's NID (245 KB JPEG) and uploads it with docType NID_FRONT. The bytes are encrypted before they reach the object store; only metadata and the SHA-256 come back.

Triggered by
CUSTOMER (own), AGENT assisting, STAFF OPS_MAKER.
Rules
  • Multipart; ≤ 5 MB; jpeg / png / pdf; the magic bytes must match the content type; ≤ 12 documents per submission (BR-W-67).
  • Envelope encryption: a random AES-256 key per document, wrapped with the master key; object key kyc/{customerId}/{documentId}; plaintext never on disk.
  • Only while DRAFT (NOT_DRAFT).
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · size, type, magic bytes, > 12 documents
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · path entity missing (or not visible to the actor)
  • 409 STATE_CONFLICT — reasons NOT_DRAFT · submission not DRAFT
Emits
AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events (KYC_DOCUMENT_UPLOADED)
Example request / response
Request
POST /v1/kyc-submissions/0192f2c0-7a00-7000-8000-0000000000b1/documents
X-Actor-Type: AGENT
X-Actor-Id: 0192f4a0-0a01-7c00-8a00-00000000a001
X-Channel: PARTNER_APP
Content-Type: multipart/form-data

docType=NID_FRONT
file=(binary) nid-front.jpg
Response 201
{
  "documentId": "0192f2d0-0000-7000-8000-0000000000d1",

  "contentType": "image/jpeg",
  "sizeBytes": 245000,

UC-027Submit KYC for reviewwalletR1
POST/v1/kyc-submissions/{submissionId}:submit
operationId submitKyc

Rahim's tier-2 data and documents are complete, so the agent taps Submit. The service validates the tier requirements, checks for a duplicate NID, calls the blacklist, scores the risk (5 points → LOW) and moves the submission to SUBMITTED, emitting KYC_SUBMITTED for compliance to screen.

Triggered by
CUSTOMER (own), AGENT assisting, STAFF OPS_MAKER.
Rules
  • Tier requirements from KYC_TIERS: every missing field / document / consent gives one errors[] entry in a 400 (BR-W-22).
  • Duplicate NID of another live customer or open submission → state EXCEPTION, item DUPLICATE_NID (BR-W-20). A blacklist hit → EXCEPTION BLACKLIST_HIT (BR-W-21).
  • Blacklist unreachable → submission proceeds with blacklistCheck PENDING; the 5-minute recheck job retries; the review gate blocks until done.
  • Risk grade by RISK_MATRIX (BR-W-31): sum of factor points, then overrides (PEP → HIGH, high-risk district ≥ MEDIUM, cash-intensive business → HIGH).
  • The KYC_SUBMITTED eventId is stored on the submission for screening correlation; a resubmission stores a new one.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · tier requirements (fields, documents, consents), DOB age, NID/DOB cross-check
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · path entity missing (or not visible to the actor)
  • 409 STATE_CONFLICT — reasons NOT_DRAFT · STATE_CONFLICT
Emits
KYC_SUBMITTED; AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events (KYC_SUBMITTED)
Example request / response
Request
POST /v1/kyc-submissions/0192f2c0-7a00-7000-8000-0000000000b1:submit
X-Actor-Type: AGENT
X-Actor-Id: 0192f4a0-0a01-7c00-8a00-00000000a001
X-Channel: PARTNER_APP
Response 200
{
  "submissionId": "0192f2c0-7a00-7000-8000-0000000000b1",
  "customerId": "0192f2a0-5b1c-7d10-8e11-0a1b2c3d4e01",
  "targetTier": 2,
  "state": "SUBMITTED",

UC-028Read a KYC submissionwalletR1
GET/v1/kyc-submissions/{submissionId}
operationId getKycSubmission

Rahim checks progress in the app and sees state: PENDING_REVIEW. A reviewer opening the same submission also sees the screening result, the risk breakdown and the document list.

Triggered by
CUSTOMER (own), AGENT assisting, any STAFF.
Rules
  • Tipping-off: CUSTOMER / AGENT callers get screeningStatus, riskGrade, riskBreakdown, eddRequired nulled / defaulted — only state is meaningful for them.
Errors

UC-031Maker proposes APPROVE / REJECTwalletR1
POST/v1/kyc-submissions/{submissionId}:decide
operationId decideKyc

Sumon compares Rahim's selfie with the NID photo, ticks the checklist and proposes APPROVE at tier 2. The submission moves to PENDING_APPROVAL and an approval request waits for an OPS_CHECKER.

Triggered by
STAFF OPS_MAKER.
Rules
  • Only from PENDING_REVIEW (NOT_PENDING_REVIEW); the review gate must still hold (GATE_NOT_SATISFIED, BR-W-23).
  • Creates KycSubmission / APPROVE_KYC or REJECT_KYC; level-1 checker OPS_CHECKER (BR-W-25).
  • HIGH risk or PEP → 2 levels (level 2 COMPLIANCE_OFFICER / CAMLCO) and EDD fields sourceOfWealth + eddNote are mandatory. An NID correction on upgrade also needs 2 levels.
  • Rejection codes: NID_MISMATCH, IMAGE_UNCLEAR, SELFIE_MISMATCH, INCOMPLETE, DUPLICATE, SCREENING_REJECT, BLACKLISTED, FRAUD_SUSPECTED, UNDERAGE, OTHER.
  • Never auto-approve: approval needs correlated CLEAR screening, blacklist done, no open exception and checker ≠ maker.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · EDD fields missing for HIGH / PEP, tier missing
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · path entity missing (or not visible to the actor)
  • 409 STATE_CONFLICT — reasons GATE_NOT_SATISFIED, NOT_PENDING_REVIEW · not in PENDING_REVIEW / gate no longer satisfied
Emits
APPROVAL_REQUESTED on approval.events; AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events (KYC_DECISION_PROPOSED); Final approval later: KYC_APPROVED, KYC_TIER_CHANGED, RISK_GRADE_CHANGED, WALLET_UPSERTED — or KYC_REJECTED
Example request / response
Request
POST /v1/kyc-submissions/0192f2c0-7a00-7000-8000-0000000000b1:decide
X-Actor-Type: STAFF
X-Actor-Id: 0192f1aa-0000-7000-8000-0000000000a1
X-Actor-Roles: OPS_MAKER
X-Channel: BACKOFFICE
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "decision": "APPROVE",
  "tier": 2,
  "reasonCode": "KYC_APPROVED",
  "checklist": {
    "nidImageLegible": true,
    "nidDataMatchesForm": true,
    "selfieMatchesNidPhoto": true,
    "ageOver18": true,
    "addressComplete": true
  },
  "comment": "Documents verified manually"
}
Response 202
{
  "submissionId": "0192f2c0-7a00-7000-8000-0000000000b1",
  "state": "PENDING_APPROVAL",
  "approvalId": "0192f400-1111-7000-8000-00000000b001",
  "requiredLevels": 1
}

UC-033Resolve an exception (OVERRIDE or REJECT)walletR1
POST/v1/kyc/exceptions/{itemId}:resolve
operationId resolveKycException

The other customer with Karim's NID is an old account being closed. Sumon requests OVERRIDE; the item stays open until an OPS_CHECKER and then a COMPLIANCE_OFFICER approve (2 levels). A REJECT would instead reject the submission at once.

Triggered by
STAFF OPS_MAKER.
Rules
  • REJECT applies immediately (submission → REJECTED, e.g. reason DUPLICATE). OVERRIDE creates ExceptionItem / OVERRIDE.
  • OVERRIDE of DUPLICATE_NID: 2 levels OPS_CHECKER + COMPLIANCE_OFFICER; BLACKLIST_HIT: checker COMPLIANCE_OFFICER.
  • Only OPEN items (NOT_OPEN); one pending override (ALREADY_PENDING). An approved override returns the submission to SUBMITTED and re-emits KYC_SUBMITTED.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · malformed header, parameter or body
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · path entity missing (or not visible to the actor)
  • 409 STATE_CONFLICT — reasons ALREADY_PENDING, NOT_OPEN · STATE_CONFLICT
Emits
APPROVAL_REQUESTED on approval.events (OVERRIDE); KYC_REJECTED (REJECT); AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events (KYC_EXCEPTION_RESOLVED)
Example request / response
Request

X-Actor-Id: 0192f1aa-0000-7000-8000-0000000000a1
X-Actor-Roles: OPS_MAKER
X-Channel: BACKOFFICE

  "resolution": "OVERRIDE",

}

Wallet closure and unclaimed balances

Close a wallet safely, pay out the rest to a same-person wallet, report long-dormant balances.

UC-034Request a wallet closurewalletR1
POST/v1/wallets/{walletId}/closures
operationId requestClosure

Karim is moving abroad and asks at a Dhaka service point to close his wallet. His NID is checked in person. He still has 425.75 Tk (42575 poisha) and chose CASH_OUT, so the closure waits in AWAITING_ZERO_BALANCE until he cashes out at Mitu Store.

Triggered by
CUSTOMER (own, X-Channel APP); STAFF SUP_MAKER, OPS_MAKER.
Rules
  • Pre-checks (BR-W-40): no open dispute / reversal request, no ledger hold and flag 8 not set, no regulatory freeze, no pending status/profile approval, zero balance; ledger unreachable = UNKNOWN (blocks).
  • Only the balance failing → AWAITING_ZERO_BALANCE; anything else failing → PRECHECK_FAILED; all pass → PENDING_APPROVAL (WalletClosure / CLOSE).
  • The balance does not block when payoutPreference.method = OWN_WALLET names a same-person wallet (same ownerId or same nidHash, credit-allowed, not P/C/F/Q/Z) (BR-W-44).
  • One open closure per wallet (CLOSURE_OPEN with existingId); closed wallet → WALLET_CLOSED. Bank payout is not available (400).
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · OWN_WALLET without toWalletId; BANK payout not available
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · path entity missing (or not visible to the actor)
  • 409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT / STATE_CONFLICT — reasons CLOSURE_OPEN, PAYOUT_TARGET_NOT_ELIGIBLE, WALLET_CLOSED — extensions existingId · STATE_CONFLICT; same Idempotency-Key, different body
Emits
Notification WALLET_CLOSURE_REQUESTED (awaitingZeroBalance true/false); APPROVAL_REQUESTED on approval.events (if all checks pass); AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events (CLOSURE_REQUESTED)
Example request / response
Request

X-Actor-Id: 0192f1aa-0000-7000-8000-0000000000a1
X-Actor-Roles: SUP_MAKER
X-Channel: BACKOFFICE
Idempotency-Key: k-requestClosure

  "requestedVia": "SERVICE_POINT",

Karim's trip is postponed. He cancels the closure; the pending approval is withdrawn.

Triggered by
CUSTOMER (own); STAFF SUP_MAKER, OPS_MAKER.
Rules
  • From PRECHECK_FAILED, AWAITING_ZERO_BALANCE or PENDING_APPROVAL only (the approval becomes CANCELLED). After approval (SETTLING onwards) it cannot be cancelled.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · malformed header, parameter or body
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · path entity missing (or not visible to the actor)
  • 409 STATE_CONFLICT — reasons INVALID_TRANSITION · STATE_CONFLICT
Emits
approval.events transition (status CANCELLED) if an approval was pending; AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events

UC-041Propose a new configuration versionwalletR1
POST/v1/wallet-config/{key}/versions
operationId proposeWalletConfig

Operations wants the closure settle window to be 20 minutes instead of 15. An OPS_MAKER proposes a new WALLET_PARAMS version; it becomes ACTIVE only after an OPS_CHECKER approves.

Triggered by
STAFF ADMIN, COMPLIANCE_OFFICER (RISK_MATRIX, KYC_TIERS, REASON_AUTHORITY, MASKING_POLICY), OPS_MAKER (WALLET_PARAMS).
Rules
  • The payload must pass the key's JSON Schema (400). One pending per key (ALREADY_PENDING).
  • Creates ConfigDocument / ACTIVATE; checker COMPLIANCE_OFFICER for RISK_MATRIX / KYC_TIERS, OPS_CHECKER otherwise. Activation reloads caches without restart.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · payload fails the key's JSON Schema
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · path entity missing (or not visible to the actor)
  • 409 STATE_CONFLICT — reasons ALREADY_PENDING · STATE_CONFLICT
Emits
APPROVAL_REQUESTED on approval.events; On approval: AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events (CONFIG_ACTIVATED)

  "payload": {},

}

UC-043Approve the current levelapprovalsR1
POST/v1/approvals/{id}:approve
operationId approveApproval

Tania checks the call recording and approves the block of Fatema's wallet. The handler re-validates and applies it in the same transaction; Fatema's wallet becomes F.

Triggered by
STAFF with a role in the current level's checkerRoles.
Rules
  • Checker ≠ maker (also compared on X-Actor-User-Id); a level-2 checker ≠ every earlier checker; ADMIN is never a checker (BR-MC-02) → 422 MAKER_CHECKER_VIOLATION.
  • The final level runs the module's ApprovalHandler: re-validation failing → REVALIDATION_FAILED; a partner stage before screening CLEAR → SCREENING_NOT_CLEAR; KYC gate → GATE_NOT_SATISFIED.
  • Not PENDING → NOT_PENDING; past 72 h → EXPIRED. For partner application stages the response carries next (the next PENDING stage).
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · malformed header, parameter or body
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · role not in the current level's checkerRoles
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · path entity missing (or not visible to the actor)
  • 409 STATE_CONFLICT — reasons EXPIRED, GATE_NOT_SATISFIED, NOT_PENDING, REVALIDATION_FAILED, SCREENING_NOT_CLEAR · not PENDING / expired / handler re-validation failed
  • 422 MAKER_CHECKER_VIOLATION · checker = maker, or decided an earlier level / stage
Emits
APPROVAL_DECIDED on approval.events; AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events (APPROVAL_DECIDED); plus whatever the approved change emits (e.g. WALLET_UPSERTED, KYC_APPROVED, PARTNER_UPSERTED)
Example request / response
Request
POST /v1/approvals/0192f400-1111-7000-8000-00000000b001:approve
X-Actor-Type: STAFF
X-Actor-Id: 0192f1aa-0000-7000-8000-0000000000a2
X-Actor-Roles: SUP_CHECKER
X-Channel: BACKOFFICE
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "comment": "Verified"
}
Response 200
{
  "approvalId": "0192f400-1111-7000-8000-00000000b001",
  "module": "wallet",
  "entityType": "WalletStatusChange",
  "entityId": "0192f400-1111-7000-8000-00000000a001",
  "action": "APPLY",
  "makerId": "0192f1aa-0000-7000-8000-0000000000a1",
  "status": "APPROVED",
  "level": 1,
  "requiredLevels": 1,
  "checkerRoles": [
    "SUP_CHECKER",
    "OPS_CHECKER"
  ],
  "amountPoisha": null,
  "summary": "Block wallet 8801812****01 (CUSTOMER_REQUEST)",
  "payload": {
    "targetStatus": "BLOCKED",
    "reasonCode": "CUSTOMER_REQUEST"
  },
  "decidedBy": [
    "0192f1aa-0000-7000-8000-0000000000a2"

UC-044Reject the current levelapprovalsR1
POST/v1/approvals/{id}:reject
operationId rejectApproval

Tania finds the NID photos unreadable in a KYC approval and rejects with a comment. The submission goes back to PENDING_REVIEW for the maker — it is not a KYC rejection.

Triggered by
STAFF with a role in the current level's checkerRoles.
Rules
  • comment required; reasonCode required for partner application stages (BR-PAR-12).
  • Effects depend on the entity: a rejected KYC proposal returns to PENDING_REVIEW (BR-W-28); a rejected partner stage rejects the application; a rejected status change does nothing to the wallet.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · malformed header, parameter or body
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · role not in the current level's checkerRoles
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · path entity missing (or not visible to the actor)
  • 409 STATE_CONFLICT — reasons EXPIRED, NOT_PENDING · STATE_CONFLICT
  • 422 MAKER_CHECKER_VIOLATION · checker = maker

Partner onboarding applications

Agents, merchants, distributors and institutions apply; staff review through an approval chain.

UC-045Create a partner application (DRAFT)partnerR1
POST/v1/partner-applications
operationId createPartnerApplication

Sales officer Rafiq (PARTNER_OPS_MAKER) starts an application for the RETAIL agent "Mitu Store" in Mirpur under the Mirpur sub-distributor. The response lists the documents still missing from the checklist.

Triggered by
STAFF PARTNER_OPS_MAKER (any type); DISTRIBUTOR for AGENT / MERCHANT in its own subtree; CUSTOMER for MERCHANT / MICRO self-registration (when the flag is on).
Rules
  • BR-PAR-01: a DISTRIBUTOR must propose itself or a descendant as parent; CUSTOMER only for MERCHANT/MICRO (else 403); self-registration off → SELF_REGISTRATION_DISABLED.
  • settlementMode BANK → 422 SERVICE_DISABLED BANK_SETTLEMENT_DISABLED (OPEN D-42).
  • Form validation: displayName 1–25 of [A-Z0-9 .&'-] (it is QR tag 59), canonical MSISDN, MCC required for merchants, GEO chain, trade licence except INSTITUTION/GOVERNMENT and MERCHANT/MICRO.
  • Drafts expire after 30 days without submit (BR-PAR-02).
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · malformed header, parameter or body
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · CUSTOMER for non-MICRO; DISTRIBUTOR proposing a parent outside its subtree
  • 409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT · same Idempotency-Key, different body
  • 422 SERVICE_DISABLED — reasons BANK_SETTLEMENT_DISABLED, SELF_REGISTRATION_DISABLED · self-registration off; settlementMode BANK
Emits
AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events (PARTNER_APPLICATION_CREATED)

UC-049Add a key personpartnerR1
POST/v1/partner-applications/{applicationId}/key-persons
operationId addKeyPerson

Mitu Akter is the proprietor of Mitu Store (100 % ownership). Rafiq adds her with NID and phone; both are blacklist-checked by hash right away.

Triggered by
STAFF PARTNER_OPS_MAKER; DISTRIBUTOR (initiator); CUSTOMER (initiator).
Rules
  • NID stored only encrypted + HMAC; the response shows nidMasked / msisdnMasked.
  • Blacklist check now (and again at submit) by valueHash: listed → 422 RISK_BLOCKED BLACKLISTED (no list details returned); check unreachable → 503, nothing changed (BR-PAR-05).
  • Only while DRAFT / RETURNED. Any key-person change later bumps the partner version (screening input).
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · malformed header, parameter or body
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · path entity missing (or not visible to the actor)
  • 409 STATE_CONFLICT — reasons INVALID_TRANSITION · application not DRAFT / RETURNED
  • 422 RISK_BLOCKED — reasons BLACKLISTED · NID or MSISDN blacklisted (no list details returned)
  • 503 DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE · blacklist check unreachable; nothing changed
Emits
AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events (KEY_PERSON_ADDED)
Example request / response
Request
POST /v1/partner-applications/0192f4b0-5a01-7c00-8a00-0000000a0001/key-persons

X-Actor-Roles: PARTNER_OPS_MAKER
X-Channel: BACKOFFICE
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "role": "PROPRIETOR",
  "nameEn": "Mitu Akter",
  "nameBn": "মিতু আক্তার",
  "nid": "1990123456789",
  "dob": "1990-02-14",
  "msisdn": "8801712345678",
  "ownershipBps": 10000,
  "pepDeclared": false
}
Response 201
{
  "keyPersonId": "0192f4b2-0001-7c00-8a00-0000000000c1",

UC-057Submit / resubmit an applicationpartnerR1
POST/v1/partner-applications/{applicationId}:submit
operationId submitPartnerApplication

Everything for Mitu Store is in place. Rafiq submits: the checklist passes, the blacklist answers "not listed", partner code A000045674 is allocated, risk is LOW (RETAIL +10), the AGENT_STD chain is chosen and stage 1 (SALES) becomes PENDING. The partner is published as PENDING_SCREENING for compliance.

Triggered by
STAFF PARTNER_OPS_MAKER; DISTRIBUTOR (initiator); CUSTOMER (initiator).
Rules
  • Checklist (BR-PAR-03): mandatory documents, ≥ 1 key person, bank account and site visit where required, Σ ownership ≤ 100 % — missing items → 400 with errors[] and missingItems.
  • No other live partner with the same MSISDN and type (DUPLICATE_PARTNER). Blacklist for every identifier (RISK_BLOCKED; unreachable → 503, nothing changed).
  • First submit: partner code (type letter + 8-digit sequence + Luhn digit), partner row PENDING_SCREENING version 1, first hierarchy assignment ACTIVE (BR-PAR-06).
  • Risk score by rules (LOW < 30 ≤ MEDIUM < 60 ≤ HIGH); HIGH adds the EDD stage. Chain by most specific match (NO_APPROVAL_CHAIN). Stages are sequential.
  • Resubmit after a return: new round from stage 1; screening re-requested only if screened fields changed; more than 3 resubmissions → MAX_RESUBMISSIONS.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED — extensions missingItems · missing mandatory documents / key persons / bank account / site visit; errors[] lists them
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · path entity missing (or not visible to the actor)
  • 409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT / STATE_CONFLICT — reasons DUPLICATE_PARTNER, INVALID_TRANSITION, MAX_RESUBMISSIONS, NO_APPROVAL_CHAIN · STATE_CONFLICT; same Idempotency-Key, different body
  • 422 RISK_BLOCKED — reasons BLACKLISTED · an identifier is blacklisted
  • 503 DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE · limits-risk unreachable; nothing changed
Emits
PARTNER_UPSERTED (PENDING_SCREENING); APPROVAL_REQUESTED on approval.events (stage 1); Notification PARTNER_APPLICATION_RECEIVED to the applicant MSISDN; AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events (PARTNER_APPLICATION_SUBMITTED)
Example request / response
Request
POST /v1/partner-applications/0192f4b0-5a01-7c00-8a00-0000000a0001:submit
X-Actor-Type: STAFF
X-Actor-Id: 0192f1aa-0000-7000-8000-0000000000a1
X-Actor-Roles: PARTNER_OPS_MAKER
X-Channel: BACKOFFICE
Idempotency-Key: k-submitPartnerApplica
Response 200
{
  "applicationId": "0192f4b0-5a01-7c00-8a00-0000000a0001",

UC-058Return an application for correctionpartnerR1
POST/v1/partner-applications/{applicationId}:return
operationId returnPartnerApplication

At the COMPLIANCE stage Nasrin cannot read the back of Mitu Akter's NID. She returns the application with DOCUMENT_UNREADABLE; Mitu Store and Rafiq both get a message.

Triggered by
STAFF with the role of the current PENDING stage.
Rules
  • Application → RETURNED; the round's stages closed (RETURNED; WAITING → CANCELLED); the partner keeps its status (BR-PAR-13).
  • The decider must not be the initiator (MAKER_CHECKER_VIOLATION); the stage must be PENDING (INVALID_TRANSITION).
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · malformed header, parameter or body
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · path entity missing (or not visible to the actor)
  • 409 STATE_CONFLICT — reasons INVALID_TRANSITION · stage not PENDING
  • 422 MAKER_CHECKER_VIOLATION · decider = initiator
Emits
Notification PARTNER_APPLICATION_RETURNED (applicant MSISDN and initiator); approval.events transition (RETURNED is published as REJECTED); AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events (PARTNER_APPLICATION_RETURNED)
Example request / response
Request
POST /v1/partner-applications/0192f4b0-5a01-7c00-8a00-0000000a0001:return
X-Actor-Type: STAFF
X-Actor-Id: 0192f1aa-0000-7000-8000-0000000000c3
X-Actor-Roles: COMPLIANCE_OFFICER
X-Channel: BACKOFFICE
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "stageNo": 2,
  "reasonCode": "DOCUMENT_UNREADABLE",

UC-069Move a partner under a new parentpartnerR1
POST/v1/partners/{partnerId}/hierarchy-changes
operationId requestHierarchyChange

From 1 November (2026-10-31T18:00Z = midnight Dhaka) Mitu Store should report to the new Mirpur-2 sub-distributor. A PARTNER_OPS_MAKER requests it; after the checker approves, the change is SCHEDULED and applied by a job at that time (see flow F7).

Triggered by
STAFF PARTNER_OPS_MAKER.
Rules
  • Checked at request and at approval (BR-PAR-30..36): new parent ACTIVE (PARENT_NOT_ACTIVE); not itself or a descendant (CYCLE); depth incl. subtree ≤ 4 (DEPTH_EXCEEDED); allowed pair (RULE_NOT_ALLOWED).
  • No backdating (BACKDATED), ≤ 180 days ahead, one SCHEDULED change per partner (ALREADY_SCHEDULED).
  • Applying recomputes ancestors for the whole subtree in chunks of 500, each partner version + 1.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · malformed header, parameter or body
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · path entity missing (or not visible to the actor)
  • 409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT / STATE_CONFLICT — reasons ALREADY_SCHEDULED, BACKDATED, CYCLE, DEPTH_EXCEEDED, PARENT_NOT_ACTIVE, RULE_NOT_ALLOWED · STATE_CONFLICT; same Idempotency-Key, different body
Emits
APPROVAL_REQUESTED on approval.events; When applied: PARTNER_UPSERTED for the partner and every descendant; AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events (PARTNER_HIERARCHY_CHANGE_REQUESTED)
Example request / response
Request

X-Channel: BACKOFFICE
Idempotency-Key: k-requestHierarchyChan
Content-Type: application/json

  "newParentPartnerId": "0192f4a0-0d04-7c00-8a00-00000000d004",

  "reasonCode": "TERRITORY_REALIGNMENT",
  "comment": "Mirpur split"
}
Response 202
{
  "approvalId": "0192f400-1111-7000-8000-00000000b001",
  "assignmentPreview": {
    "newDepth": 4,
    "subtreeSize": 0
  }
}

Partner lifecycle and changes

Suspend, reactivate, terminate, change category / territory / profile, bank accounts, risk flags.

UC-071Suspend / reactivate / request termination of a partnerpartnerR1
POST/v1/partners/{partnerId}/status-changes
operationId changePartnerStatus

Customers complain that Mitu Store often has no cash. Her distributor (an ancestor) suspends her for 3 days with DISTRIBUTOR_SUSPENSION and until — applied at once, and her wallet is blocked in the same transaction. A staff suspension would instead return 202 and wait for a checker.

Triggered by
SERVICE compliance-service (immediate suspensions); STAFF PARTNER_OPS_MAKER (maker-checker); DISTRIBUTOR ancestor (DST-008).
Rules
  • SERVICE compliance-service: SUSPENDED with AML_FREEZE / FRAUD_FREEZE / RISK_PERFORMANCE → immediate 200; other SERVICE requests → 403; reactivating such a suspension needs STAFF maker-checker with checker COMPLIANCE_OFFICER (BR-PAR-42).
  • DISTRIBUTOR ancestor: SUSPENDED with until ≤ now + 7 days, immediate; may lift only its own suspension; SuspensionExpiryJob reactivates at until (BR-PAR-43).
  • PENDING_TERMINATION needs zero non-terminated descendants (HAS_ACTIVE_DESCENDANTS + activeDescendants) and revokes all QR codes (BR-PAR-45).
  • Wallet sync in the same transaction (BR-PAR-47): SUSPENDED → wallet BLOCKED (PARTNER_SUSPENDED); back to ACTIVE → lift only that restriction (PARTNER_REACTIVATED). If the wallet module rejects, everything rolls back.
  • Transitions only per the state machine (INVALID_TRANSITION); suspension does not cascade to descendants.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · malformed header, parameter or body
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · path entity missing (or not visible to the actor)
  • 409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT / STATE_CONFLICT — reasons ALREADY_PENDING, HAS_ACTIVE_DESCENDANTS, INVALID_TRANSITION — extensions activeDescendants, changeRequestId · activeDescendants with HAS_ACTIVE_DESCENDANTS; same Idempotency-Key, different body
Emits
PARTNER_UPSERTED; WALLET_UPSERTED (wallet restriction); Notification PARTNER_SUSPENDED / PARTNER_REACTIVATED (partner wallet + parent wallet for DST-008); APPROVAL_REQUESTED on approval.events (staff, 202); AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events (PARTNER_STATUS_CHANGE_REQUESTED|APPLIED)

X-Channel: PARTNER_APP
Idempotency-Key: k-changePartnerStatus

  "targetStatus": "SUSPENDED",

  "comment": "Cash shortage complaints"
}
Response 200 — applied

UC-072Complete a termination (zero-balance check)partnerR1
POST/v1/partners/{partnerId}:complete-termination
operationId completePartnerTermination

Dhaka Bazar is closing down. After its float was moved out, a PARTNER_OPS_CHECKER completes the termination. The ledger shows balance 0 and holds 0, so the partner becomes TERMINATED and the wallet is blocked with PARTNER_TERMINATED.

Triggered by
STAFF PARTNER_OPS_CHECKER.
Rules
  • Partner must be PENDING_TERMINATION (INVALID_TRANSITION). Reads the ledger balance: ledgerBalancePoisha and heldPoisha must both be 0 → else BALANCE_NOT_ZERO with both amounts; ledger down → 503 (BR-PAR-46).
  • Wallet → BLOCKED (PARTNER_TERMINATED) in the same transaction; wallet closure then follows the normal closure flow.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · malformed header, parameter or body
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 404 NOT_FOUND · path entity missing (or not visible to the actor)
  • 409 STATE_CONFLICT — reasons BALANCE_NOT_ZERO, INVALID_TRANSITION — extensions heldPoisha, ledgerBalancePoisha · float not settled
  • 503 DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE · ledger unreachable
Emits
PARTNER_UPSERTED (TERMINATED); WALLET_UPSERTED; Notification PARTNER_TERMINATED; AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events (PARTNER_TERMINATED)

UC-075Partner profile changes (per-field policy)partnerR1

  • 422 RISK_BLOCKED — reasons BLACKLISTED · new MSISDN blacklisted
  • 503 DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE · blacklist unreachable

Bangla QR

Generate, list, print, revoke and decode EMVCo merchant-presented QR codes.

UC-087Decode a Bangla QR (on-us)partnerR1
POST/v1/qr/decode
operationId decodeQr

Rahim scans the sticker of Dhaka Bazar's outlet OUT0001. The decoder checks the TLV structure and CRC, sees our GUID BD.COM.BITSPAY, finds the partner by account code and compares the SHA-256 of the whole payload with the stored one: onUs: true, payable: true, with the merchant wallet id.

Triggered by
CUSTOMER, AGENT, MERCHANT; any STAFF; any SERVICE.
Rules
  • Trusts the database, not the QR text (ADR-16, BR-PAR-75/76): static by account code + stored payload hash, dynamic by reference + payload hash. A changed amount with a valid CRC → payable: false, TAMPERED.
  • Malformed payloads → 400 with errors[{field: payload, message}], message ∈ CRC_MISMATCH, BAD_TLV, MISSING_TAG, BAD_FORMAT (BR-PAR-73).
  • Off-us (another GUID) → parsed but onUs=false, payable=false, OFF_US_NOT_SUPPORTED; currency ≠ 050 → CURRENCY_NOT_SUPPORTED; dynamic past expiry → EXPIRED; partner not active → PARTNER_NOT_ACTIVE.
  • Amounts via BigDecimal only (never floating point). p99 ≤ 30 ms with a Caffeine cache.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · errors[{field: payload, message ∈ CRC_MISMATCH|BAD_TLV|MISSING_TAG|BAD_FORMAT}]
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
Emits
nothing (read-only)
Example request / response
Request
POST /v1/qr/decode
X-Actor-Type: CUSTOMER
X-Actor-Id: 0192f2a0-5b1c-7d10-8e11-0a1b2c3d4e01
X-Channel: APP
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "payload": "00020101021126370014BD.COM.BITSPAY0110M0001234550201M5204541153030505802BD5911DHAKA BAZAR6005DHAKA62110707OUT000163042B98"
}
Response 200
{
  "valid": true,
  "onUs": true,
  "payable": true,
  "notPayableReason": null,
  "pointOfInitiation": "STATIC",
  "qrPayloadId": "0192f4c0-0000-7000-8000-000000000001",
  "partnerType": "MERCHANT",
  "partnerId": "0192f4a0-0e01-7c00-8a00-00000000e001",
  "partnerCode": "M000123455",
  "merchantWalletId": "0192f4a1-0e01-7c00-8a00-00000000e001",
  "agentWalletId": null,
  "displayName": "DHAKA BAZAR",
  "displayNameBn": "ঢাকা বাজার",
  "mcc": "5411",
  "city": "DHAKA",
  "amountPoisha": null,
  "reference": null,
  "billNumber": null,
  "outletCode": "OUT0001",
  "currency": "BDT",
  "expiresAt": null,
  "unknownTags": {}
}

UC-089(Re)generate a static QRpartnerR1
POST/v1/qr/static
operationId generateStaticQr

Dhaka Bazar's sticker for outlet OUT0001 was damaged. It generates a new static QR; the old one is revoked in the same transaction, so old stickers stop working.

Triggered by
MERCHANT (self), AGENT (self); STAFF PARTNER_OPS_MAKER.
Rules
  • Revokes the previous ACTIVE static QR of the same (partner, outlet) (BR-PAR-79). Partner must be ACTIVE (PARTNER_NOT_ACTIVE); outlet not closed (OUTLET_CLOSED).
  • Payload per the EMVCo MPM table: tag 01 = 11 (static), 26 = Bits Pay template, 52 MCC, 53 050, 58 BD, 59 display name, 60 city, 62.07 outlet code, 63 CRC-16/CCITT-FALSE.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · malformed header, parameter or body
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT / STATE_CONFLICT — reasons OUTLET_CLOSED, PARTNER_NOT_ACTIVE · STATE_CONFLICT; same Idempotency-Key, different body
Emits
AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events (QR_GENERATED)

X-Actor-Id: 0192f4a0-0e01-7c00-8a00-00000000e001

Idempotency-Key: k-generateStaticQr
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "partnerId": "0192f4a0-0e01-7c00-8a00-00000000e001",
  "outletId": "0192f4c0-0e01-7c00-8a00-0000000e0001"
}
Response 201
{
  "qrPayloadId": "0192f4c2-0e01-7c00-8a00-0000000e0101",
  "partnerId": "0192f4a0-0e01-7c00-8a00-00000000e001",
  "outletId": "0192f4c0-0e01-7c00-8a00-0000000e0001",
  "qrType": "STATIC",
  "payload": "00020101021226370014BD.COM.BITSPAY0110M0001234550201M5204541153030505406350.005802BD5911DHAKA BAZAR6005DHAKA62370108INV-10010510Q7K3M9QX2A0707OUT000163049491",

UC-090Generate a dynamic QR (amount + reference)partnerR1
POST/v1/qr/dynamic
operationId generateDynamicQr

Rahim buys groceries for 350 Tk at Dhaka Bazar. The cashier creates a dynamic QR with amountPoisha 35000, bill INV-1001, valid for 15 minutes; Rahim scans it and the amount is pre-filled.

Triggered by
MERCHANT (self).
Rules
  • Amount > 0 and ≤ max; expiry between 60 s and 86,400 s (default 900 s); bill number ≤ 25 ASCII.
  • Reference Q + 9 Crockford base32 characters (tag 62.05), unique. Reusable until expiry (single use not enforced).
  • Tag 54 amount = taka with exactly 2 decimals, e.g. 350.00. Dynamic payloads are never logged at INFO.
Errors
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED · amount ≤ 0 or > max, bill number, expiry outside [60, max]
  • 403 ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED · actor type, role or caller not permitted
  • 409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT / STATE_CONFLICT — reasons OUTLET_CLOSED, PARTNER_NOT_ACTIVE · STATE_CONFLICT; same Idempotency-Key, different body
Emits
AUDIT_RECORDED on audit.events (QR_GENERATED)

UC-092Printable QR imagepartnerR1
